Controlled Repository Reality Discovery

This is not a new roadmap step. It is the first evidence-collection execution unit inside the fixed Phase-19 Step-01.

We will first collect the repository baseline in one deterministic, read-only pass.

1. What this inspection will establish

The script will inspect:

repository identity;
branch, HEAD and tree;
working-tree state;
pnpm workspace/package ownership;
relevant package dependencies;
actual Phase-19 source surfaces;
Workspace Foundation;
Workspace Runtime;
Workspace Infrastructure;
Workbench;
Shell integration;
Docking architecture;
Dockview usage;
Phase-18 provider integration;
State and Event integration;
window-related existing implementation;
layout-related implementation;
persistence and recovery;
public APIs;
known workspace dependency-cycle source relationship;
relevant imports and consumers.

It will not modify source code.

EXECUTE

Run exactly:

cd ~/corei/ui/corei-os

cat > /tmp/stage25-phase19-step01-repository-discovery.sh <<'EOF'
#!/usr/bin/env bash
set -euo pipefail

ROOT="$(pwd)"
OUT="/tmp/stage25-phase19-step01-repository-discovery.txt"

: > "$OUT"

section() {
    printf '\n\n================================================================================\n' | tee -a "$OUT"
    printf '%s\n' "$1" | tee -a "$OUT"
    printf '================================================================================\n' | tee -a "$OUT"
}

run() {
    printf '\n$ %s\n' "$*" | tee -a "$OUT"
    "$@" 2>&1 | tee -a "$OUT" || true
}

echo "COREI" | tee -a "$OUT"
echo "STAGE-25 → PHASE-19 → STEP-01" | tee -a "$OUT"
echo "PHASE ENTRY, REPOSITORY REALITY & ARCHITECTURE RECONCILIATION" | tee -a "$OUT"
echo "EXECUTION UNIT: CONTROLLED READ-ONLY REPOSITORY DISCOVERY" | tee -a "$OUT"
echo "ROOT=$ROOT" | tee -a "$OUT"
echo "TIMESTAMP_UTC=$(date -u +'%Y-%m-%dT%H:%M:%SZ')" | tee -a "$OUT"

section "01 — REPOSITORY IDENTITY"

run git rev-parse --show-toplevel
run git branch --show-current
run git rev-parse HEAD
run git rev-parse HEAD^{tree}
run git status --short
run git status --branch --short

section "02 — REPOSITORY AND WORKSPACE OWNERSHIP"

printf '\nCurrent directory:\n' | tee -a "$OUT"
pwd | tee -a "$OUT"

printf '\nNearest package.json files:\n' | tee -a "$OUT"
find .. -maxdepth 3 -name package.json -type f -print 2>/dev/null \
    | sort \
    | tee -a "$OUT"

printf '\nNearest pnpm-workspace.yaml files:\n' | tee -a "$OUT"
find ../.. -maxdepth 4 -name pnpm-workspace.yaml -type f -print 2>/dev/null \
    | sort \
    | tee -a "$OUT"

printf '\nCurrent package identity:\n' | tee -a "$OUT"
if [[ -f package.json ]]; then
    node -e '
        const p=require("./package.json");
        console.log(JSON.stringify({
            name:p.name,
            version:p.version,
            private:p.private,
            type:p.type,
            dependencies:p.dependencies,
            devDependencies:p.devDependencies
        }, null, 2))
    ' 2>&1 | tee -a "$OUT"
fi

section "03 — PHASE-19 RELEVANT TOP-LEVEL SOURCE REALITY"

for path in \
    src/workbench \
    src/workspaces \
    src/workspace \
    src/providers \
    src/capabilities \
    src/state \
    src/events \
    src/shell \
    src/app \
    src/docking \
    src/layout \
    src/layouts \
    src/windowing \
    src/runtime \
    src/platform-runtime \
    src/platform \
    src/kernel
do
    if [[ -e "$path" ]]; then
        echo "[EXISTS] $path" | tee -a "$OUT"
    else
        echo "[ABSENT] $path" | tee -a "$OUT"
    fi
done

section "04 — RELEVANT SOURCE FILE INVENTORY"

find src \
    -type f \
    \( \
        -iname '*workspace*' -o \
        -iname '*workbench*' -o \
        -iname '*window*' -o \
        -iname '*dock*' -o \
        -iname '*layout*' -o \
        -iname '*persist*' -o \
        -iname '*serial*' -o \
        -iname '*restore*' -o \
        -iname '*recovery*' -o \
        -iname '*provider*' -o \
        -iname '*capability*' \
    \) \
    -print 2>/dev/null \
    | sort \
    | tee -a "$OUT"

section "05 — WORKBENCH TREE"

if [[ -d src/workbench ]]; then
    find src/workbench -type f -print \
        | sort \
        | tee -a "$OUT"
else
    echo "src/workbench ABSENT" | tee -a "$OUT"
fi

section "06 — WORKSPACES TREE"

if [[ -d src/workspaces ]]; then
    find src/workspaces -type f -print \
        | sort \
        | tee -a "$OUT"
else
    echo "src/workspaces ABSENT" | tee -a "$OUT"
fi

section "07 — SINGULAR WORKSPACE TREE"

if [[ -d src/workspace ]]; then
    find src/workspace -type f -print \
        | sort \
        | tee -a "$OUT"
else
    echo "src/workspace ABSENT" | tee -a "$OUT"
fi

section "08 — DOCKING TREE"

if [[ -d src/docking ]]; then
    find src/docking -type f -print \
        | sort \
        | tee -a "$OUT"
else
    echo "src/docking ABSENT" | tee -a "$OUT"
fi

section "09 — WINDOWING TREE"

if [[ -d src/windowing ]]; then
    find src/windowing -type f -print \
        | sort \
        | tee -a "$OUT"
else
    echo "src/windowing ABSENT" | tee -a "$OUT"
fi

section "10 — PROVIDER AND CAPABILITY RELEVANT FILES"

find src/providers src/capabilities \
    -type f \
    \( \
        -iname '*workspace*' -o \
        -iname '*workbench*' -o \
        -iname '*window*' -o \
        -iname '*dock*' -o \
        -iname '*layout*' \
    \) \
    -print 2>/dev/null \
    | sort \
    | tee -a "$OUT"

section "11 — DOCKVIEW PACKAGE AND SOURCE USAGE"

printf '\nPackage references:\n' | tee -a "$OUT"
grep -RIn \
    --exclude-dir=node_modules \
    --exclude-dir=dist \
    --exclude-dir=.git \
    -E '"dockview"|dockview-core|dockview-react' \
    package.json pnpm-lock.yaml 2>/dev/null \

# COREI — Stage-25 Phase-19 Step-01
## Architecture Reconciliation

## 1. Repository Reality

Phase-19 is not entering a greenfield repository.

Existing implementation already includes:

- Workbench architecture
- Workspace architecture
- Docking architecture
- Layout architecture
- Persistence infrastructure
- Event infrastructure
- Bounded state infrastructure
- Windowing infrastructure
- Capability infrastructure
- Provider infrastructure
- Institutional Shell integration
- Dockview integration

Phase-19 shall therefore operate primarily through:

- integration;
- reconciliation;
- completion;
- operationalization;
- validation.

Phase-19 shall not assume that an existing capability is missing merely
because another directory name or abstraction name exists.

---

## 2. Active Runtime Composition

The strongest discovered active center-surface composition is:

    Institutional Shell
        ↓
    ShellLayout
        ↓
    CenterRegion
        ↓
    WorkspaceHost
        ↓
    WorkbenchCenter
        ↓
    DockviewAdapter
        ↓
    Dockview

The strongest current Workbench execution authority is:

    src/workbench

The existing Dockview technology boundary is:

    src/foundation/workspace

---

## 3. Workspace Architecture Reality

Multiple workspace architecture families exist:

    src/experience/workspace
    src/workspace
    src/workspaces
    src/workbench/workspace
    src/workbench/workspaces

These shall not be assumed to be co-equal runtime authorities.

Step-01 does not authorize:

- deletion;
- migration;
- consolidation;
- renaming;
- removal.

Any later modification requires explicit ownership evidence and authorization
from the applicable Phase-19 step.

---

## 4. Docking Architecture Reality

Existing docking-related surfaces include:

    src/docking
    src/workbench/docking
    src/workspaces/docking
    src/foundation/workspace

Current architectural interpretation:

    src/foundation/workspace
        Dockview technology integration boundary.

    src/workbench/docking
        Workbench-level docking behavior and runtime integration.

    src/workspaces/docking
        Workspace-domain docking contracts and composition.

    src/docking
        Existing generic docking subsystem requiring explicit classification
        before modification.

Phase-19 shall not create a competing docking platform.

---

## 5. Layout Architecture Reality

Existing layout surfaces include:

    src/shell/layout
    src/workbench/layout
    src/workspaces/layout
    src/navigation/layout
    src/viewport/layout

These may represent distinct scopes:

    Shell Layout
        Application frame geometry.

    Workbench Layout
        Operator workspace composition.

    Workspace Layout
        Workspace-domain layout model.

    Navigation Layout
        Navigation-specific composition.

    Viewport Layout
        Viewport behavior and geometry.

Phase-19 shall preserve architectural scope boundaries.

---

## 6. Persistence Architecture Reality

Existing persistence surfaces include:

    src/workbench/workspace/persistence
    src/workspaces/persistence
    src/docking/persistence
    src/windowing/persistence
    src/shell/persistence

The existing Workbench workspace persistence implementation already includes:

- snapshot contracts;
- snapshot builder;
- serializer;
- deserializer;
- storage;
- persistence runtime.

Phase-19 shall not create a competing workspace persistence subsystem.

---

## 7. Event Architecture Reality

The absence of a top-level src/events directory does not prove that event
infrastructure is missing.

The Workbench already contains:

    src/workbench/events/event-bus.ts
    src/workbench/events/event-contract.ts

Phase-19 shall consume or extend the correct existing event authority only
when explicitly required by the applicable step.

---

## 8. State Architecture Reality

State is currently bounded by subsystem ownership.

Existing Zustand-backed state exists across Workbench, docking, activity,
sidebar, tabs, panel focus, and windowing concerns.

The absence of a single global state directory does not authorize creation of
a competing global state platform.

---

## 9. Windowing Architecture Reality

A pre-existing windowing subsystem exists:

    src/windowing

It contains:

- components;
- contracts;
- hooks;
- lifecycle;
- models;
- persistence;
- providers;
- registry;
- runtime;
- state.

Existence is established.

Full active integration into the Shell → WorkspaceHost → Workbench execution
chain is not yet assumed.

Phase-19 shall validate existing windowing ownership and integration before
extending or replacing it.

---

## 10. Phase-18 Provider Integration Boundary

Existing capability-provider integration already covers:

- capability-provider binding;
- provider resolution;
- lifecycle integration;
- runtime integration;
- operational observation;
- health evaluation;
- availability evaluation;
- operational eligibility.

Phase-19 shall consume this architecture.

Phase-19 shall not create a competing provider-resolution or
capability-provider integration mechanism.

---

## 11. Protected Architecture

The following are protected unless an explicit Phase-19 step authorizes a
bounded change:

    src/kernel
    src/platform
    src/platform-runtime
    src/runtime
    src/platform-bootstrap
    src/platform-configuration
    src/platform-registry

    src/providers
    src/capabilities/integration

    src/shell

    src/foundation/workspace

---

## 12. Phase-19 Engineering Law

Phase-19 is primarily an:

    INTEGRATION
    RECONCILIATION
    COMPLETION
    OPERATIONALIZATION
    VALIDATION

phase.

It is not automatically authorized as a:

    GREENFIELD REBUILD
    ARCHITECTURE REPLACEMENT
    LEGACY DELETION
    SUBSYSTEM DUPLICATION
    PLATFORM KERNEL RECONSTRUCTION
    PROVIDER PLATFORM RECONSTRUCTION
    INSTITUTIONAL SHELL RECONSTRUCTION

phase.

---

## 13. Step-01 Closure Decision

Repository reality has been established.

Architectural overlap has been identified.

Protected boundaries have been identified.

The pre-implementation source baseline is captured separately by the Step-01
baseline manifest.

Subsequent implementation may proceed only through the fixed Phase-19
execution sequence and only within the change boundary authorized by each
individual step.
DOC

STAGE-25 → PHASE-19 → STEP-02
Window & Workspace Platform Contract Boundary

We proceed only to Step-02 and preserve the fixed Phase-19 execution flow.

Step-01 is now:

COMPLETE
CERTIFIED
CLOSED

Step-02 is the first controlled implementation step, but its scope is deliberately narrow:

Define the authoritative Phase-19 Window & Workspace Platform contract boundary before operational coordination, window management, Dockview runtime integration, layout runtime, persistence, restoration, or later capabilities are implemented.

1. Step-02 Objective

Establish a small, stable, provider-independent contract surface defining what Phase-19 coordinates.

Step-02 will define:

platform identity;
workspace identity;
window identity;
workspace/window references;
lifecycle vocabulary;
platform context;
platform snapshot;
coordination contract;
public API.

Step-02 will not implement:

workspace operational coordination — Step-03;
window manager/runtime behavior — Step-04;
Dockview runtime integration — Step-05;
layout runtime — Step-06;
split/tab/group operations — Step-07;
floating windows — Step-08;
browser multi-window runtime — Step-09;
multi-monitor behavior — Step-10;
focus/presentation modes — Step-11;
workspace switching — Step-12;
persistence — Step-13;
restoration/recovery — Step-14;
shell integration changes — Step-15;
event/state coordination — Step-16;
diagnostics — Step-17.
2. Architecture Decision

Step-01 established multiple existing implementation families. Therefore Step-02 must not place the new Phase-19 cross-cutting contract inside one existing implementation family such as:

src/workbench
src/workspaces
src/windowing
src/docking

Doing so would prematurely make one subsystem the owner of the entire Phase-19 platform.

The bounded Phase-19 platform contract will therefore be introduced at:

src/window-workspace-platform/

This is not a replacement subsystem.

It is the Phase-19 coordination boundary above existing implementations:

Window & Workspace Platform Contract
        │
        ├── consumes existing Workspace authority
        ├── consumes existing Workbench authority
        ├── consumes existing Windowing authority
        ├── consumes existing Docking authority
        ├── consumes existing Layout authority
        └── consumes existing Provider boundaries

The contract remains:

provider-independent;
React-independent;
Dockview-independent;
Zustand-independent;
persistence-independent;
shell-independent.

This prevents Step-02 from prematurely implementing later steps.

3. Authorized Files

Step-02 creates only:

src/window-workspace-platform/
├── contracts/
│   ├── window-workspace-platform.types.ts
│   ├── window-workspace-platform.contract.ts
│   └── index.ts
└── index.ts

No existing application source file is modified.

No protected boundary is modified.

4. Execute Step-02 Implementation

Run the following block exactly once:

cd ~/corei/ui/corei-os

cat > /tmp/stage25-phase19-step02-implement.sh <<'EOF'
#!/usr/bin/env bash

# ==============================================================================
# COREI — STAGE-25 / PHASE-19 / STEP-02
# ==============================================================================
#
# Step:
#   Window & Workspace Platform Contract Boundary
#
# Purpose:
#   Establish the provider-independent, implementation-independent contract
#   surface for Phase-19 Window & Workspace Platform coordination.
#
# Scope:
#   - Contract types
#   - Platform contract
#   - Public API
#
# Explicitly Not Implemented:
#   - Workspace operational coordination
#   - Window management runtime
#   - Dockview runtime integration
#   - Layout runtime
#   - Split / tab / group management
#   - Floating window management
#   - Multi-window runtime
#   - Multi-monitor runtime
#   - Presentation modes
#   - Workspace switching
#   - Persistence
#   - Restoration / recovery
#   - Shell integration changes
#   - Event / state coordination
#   - Diagnostics
#
# ==============================================================================

set -euo pipefail

ROOT="$(pwd)"
BASELINE="${ROOT}/stage25-phase19-step01/PHASE19_BASELINE_MANIFEST.sha256"
TARGET="${ROOT}/src/window-workspace-platform"

echo "============================================================================"
echo "COREI — STAGE-25 → PHASE-19 → STEP-02"
echo "WINDOW & WORKSPACE PLATFORM CONTRACT BOUNDARY"
echo "============================================================================"
echo
echo "ROOT=${ROOT}"
echo

# ------------------------------------------------------------------------------
# 01 — PRECONDITION VALIDATION
# ------------------------------------------------------------------------------

if [[ ! -f "${BASELINE}" ]]; then
    echo "[FAIL] Step-01 baseline manifest not found."
    exit 1
fi

if ! grep -q "STATUS: CERTIFIED COMPLETE" \
    "${ROOT}/stage25-phase19-step01/PHASE19_STEP01_CERTIFICATION.md"; then
    echo "[FAIL] Step-01 certification is not complete."
    exit 1
fi

if [[ -e "${TARGET}" ]]; then
    echo "[FAIL] Target already exists: ${TARGET}"
    echo "Stop and reconcile before continuing."
    exit 1
fi

echo "[PASS] Step-01 certification confirmed."
echo "[PASS] Step-01 baseline manifest confirmed."
echo "[PASS] Step-02 target does not already exist."

# ------------------------------------------------------------------------------
# 02 — CREATE CONTRACT DIRECTORY
# ------------------------------------------------------------------------------

mkdir -p "${TARGET}/contracts"

# ------------------------------------------------------------------------------
# 03 — PLATFORM TYPES
# ------------------------------------------------------------------------------

cat > "${TARGET}/contracts/window-workspace-platform.types.ts" <<'TS'
/**
 * =============================================================================
 * COREI — INSTITUTIONAL ALGORITHMIC TRADING PLATFORM
 * =============================================================================
 *
 * File:
 *   window-workspace-platform.types.ts
 *
 * Stage:
 *   Stage-25 — Platform Enablement
 *
 * Phase:
 *   Phase-19 — Window & Workspace Platform
 *
 * Step:
 *   Step-02 — Window & Workspace Platform Contract Boundary
 *
 * Purpose:
 *   Defines the provider-independent identity, lifecycle, reference, context,
 *   and snapshot vocabulary for the Window & Workspace Platform.
 *
 * Architectural Rules:
 *   - No React dependency.
 *   - No Dockview dependency.
 *   - No Zustand dependency.
 *   - No persistence implementation dependency.
 *   - No Shell implementation dependency.
 *   - No provider implementation dependency.
 *
 * =============================================================================
 */

/* =============================================================================
 * SECTION 01 — BRANDED IDENTITIES
 * =============================================================================
 */

declare const windowWorkspacePlatformIdBrand: unique symbol;
declare const workspaceIdBrand: unique symbol;
declare const windowIdBrand: unique symbol;

export type WindowWorkspacePlatformId = string & {
  readonly [windowWorkspacePlatformIdBrand]: true;
};

export type WorkspaceId = string & {
  readonly [workspaceIdBrand]: true;
};

export type WindowId = string & {
  readonly [windowIdBrand]: true;
};

/* =============================================================================
 * SECTION 02 — LIFECYCLE VOCABULARY
 * =============================================================================
 */

export type WindowWorkspacePlatformLifecycleState =
  | "idle"
  | "starting"
  | "running"
  | "stopping"
  | "stopped"
  | "failed";

export type WorkspaceLifecycleState =
  | "registered"
  | "inactive"
  | "activating"
  | "active"
  | "deactivating"
  | "failed";

export type WindowLifecycleState =
  | "registered"
  | "opening"
  | "open"
  | "closing"
  | "closed"
  | "failed";

/* =============================================================================
 * SECTION 03 — PLATFORM REFERENCES
 * =============================================================================
 */

export interface WorkspaceReference {
  readonly workspaceId: WorkspaceId;
  readonly lifecycleState: WorkspaceLifecycleState;
}

export interface WindowReference {
  readonly windowId: WindowId;
  readonly workspaceId: WorkspaceId | null;
  readonly lifecycleState: WindowLifecycleState;
}

/* =============================================================================
 * SECTION 04 — PLATFORM CONTEXT
 * =============================================================================
 */

export interface WindowWorkspacePlatformContext {
  readonly platformId: WindowWorkspacePlatformId;
  readonly lifecycleState: WindowWorkspacePlatformLifecycleState;
  readonly activeWorkspaceId: WorkspaceId | null;
  readonly activeWindowId: WindowId | null;
}

/* =============================================================================
 * SECTION 05 — PLATFORM SNAPSHOT
 * =============================================================================
 */

export interface WindowWorkspacePlatformSnapshot {
  readonly context: WindowWorkspacePlatformContext;
  readonly workspaces: readonly WorkspaceReference[];
  readonly windows: readonly WindowReference[];
}
TS

# ------------------------------------------------------------------------------
# 04 — PLATFORM CONTRACT

cat > "${TARGET}/contracts/window-workspace-platform.contract.ts" <<'TS'
/**
 * =============================================================================
 * COREI — INSTITUTIONAL ALGORITHMIC TRADING PLATFORM
 * =============================================================================
 *
 * File:
 *   window-workspace-platform.contract.ts
 *
 * Stage:
 *   Stage-25 — Platform Enablement
 *
 * Phase:
 *   Phase-19 — Window & Workspace Platform
 *
 * Step:
 *   Step-02 — Window & Workspace Platform Contract Boundary
 *
 * Purpose:
 *   Defines the authoritative provider-independent coordination contract for
 *   the Window & Workspace Platform.
 *
 * Important:
 *   This contract defines the platform boundary only.
 *
 *   It does not implement:
 *   - workspace coordination;
 *   - window management;
 *   - Dockview integration;
 *   - layout operations;
 *   - persistence;
 *   - restoration;
 *   - recovery;
 *   - events;
 *   - diagnostics.
 *
 * =============================================================================
 */

import type {
  WindowId,
  WindowReference,
  WindowWorkspacePlatformContext,
  WindowWorkspacePlatformLifecycleState,
  WindowWorkspacePlatformSnapshot,
  WorkspaceId,
  WorkspaceReference,
} from "./window-workspace-platform.types";

/* =============================================================================
 * SECTION 01 — PLATFORM CONTRACT
 * =============================================================================
 */

export interface WindowWorkspacePlatformContract {
  /**
   * Returns the current platform lifecycle state.
   */
  getLifecycleState(): WindowWorkspacePlatformLifecycleState;

  /**
   * Returns the current immutable platform context.
   */
  getContext(): WindowWorkspacePlatformContext;

  /**
   * Returns the current immutable platform snapshot.
   */
  getSnapshot(): WindowWorkspacePlatformSnapshot;

  /**
   * Returns all workspaces currently known to the platform boundary.
   */
  getWorkspaces(): readonly WorkspaceReference[];

  /**
   * Returns a workspace reference when known.
   */
  getWorkspace(workspaceId: WorkspaceId): WorkspaceReference | null;

  /**
   * Returns all windows currently known to the platform boundary.
   */
  getWindows(): readonly WindowReference[];

  /**
   * Returns a window reference when known.
   */
  getWindow(windowId: WindowId): WindowReference | null;

  /**
   * Returns the active workspace identity when one exists.
   */
  getActiveWorkspaceId(): WorkspaceId | null;

  /**
   * Returns the active window identity when one exists.
   */
  getActiveWindowId(): WindowId | null;
}
TS

1. Step-03 Objective

Establish deterministic workspace operational coordination for the Phase-19 platform boundary.

The coordinator will own:

registration of workspace references into the Phase-19 coordination boundary;
removal of known workspace references;
workspace lifecycle-state updates;
active-workspace coordination;
deterministic immutable workspace snapshots;
workspace lookup;
duplicate-registration protection;
unknown-workspace protection;
active-workspace cleanup when a workspace is removed.

It will not own:

the existing workspace runtime;
workspace loading;
Dockview;
window management;
layout operations;
persistence;
restoration;
recovery;
events;
Zustand;
React;
provider resolution;
Shell integration.

        │
        │ future controlled adapters/integration
        ▼
Workspace Operational Coordinator
        │
        ▼
Phase-19 Contract Boundary
        │
        ▼
Immutable Workspace References
and Active Workspace Identity

This coordinator is a coordination authority, not a replacement workspace runtime.

3. Step-03 Contract Semantics

#!/usr/bin/env bash

# ==============================================================================
# COREI — STAGE-25 / PHASE-19 / STEP-03
# ==============================================================================
#
# Step:
#   Workspace Operational Coordination
#
# Purpose:
#   Establish deterministic workspace coordination behind the Phase-19
#   Window & Workspace Platform contract boundary.
#
# Scope:
#   - Workspace operational coordinator contract
#   - Workspace operational coordinator implementation
#   - Public API exposure
#
# Explicitly Not Implemented:
#   - Window management
#   - Dockview runtime integration
#   - Layout runtime
#   - Split / tab / group management
#   - Floating windows
#   - Browser multi-window runtime
#   - Multi-monitor runtime
#   - Persistence
#   - Restoration
#   - Recovery
#   - Event publication
#   - Diagnostics
#   - Shell integration
#
# ==============================================================================

set -euo pipefail

ROOT="$(pwd)"
TARGET="${ROOT}/src/window-workspace-platform"

CONTRACT_FILE="${TARGET}/contracts/workspace-operational-coordinator.contract.ts"
COORDINATION_DIR="${TARGET}/coordination"
COORDINATOR_FILE="${COORDINATION_DIR}/workspace-operational-coordinator.ts"
COORDINATION_INDEX="${COORDINATION_DIR}/index.ts"
CONTRACTS_INDEX="${TARGET}/contracts/index.ts"
PLATFORM_INDEX="${TARGET}/index.ts"

echo "============================================================================"
echo "COREI — STAGE-25 → PHASE-19 → STEP-03"
echo "WORKSPACE OPERATIONAL COORDINATION"
echo "============================================================================"
echo
echo "ROOT=${ROOT}"
echo

# ------------------------------------------------------------------------------
# 01 — PRECONDITION VALIDATION
# ------------------------------------------------------------------------------

if [[ ! -d "${TARGET}" ]]; then
    echo "[FAIL] Step-02 platform contract boundary does not exist."
    exit 1
fi

if [[ ! -f "${TARGET}/contracts/window-workspace-platform.types.ts" ]]; then
    echo "[FAIL] Step-02 platform types are missing."
    exit 1
fi

if [[ ! -f "${TARGET}/contracts/window-workspace-platform.contract.ts" ]]; then
    echo "[FAIL] Step-02 platform contract is missing."
    exit 1
fi

if [[ ! -f "${CONTRACTS_INDEX}" ]]; then
    echo "[FAIL] Step-02 contracts public API is missing."
    exit 1
fi

if [[ ! -f "${PLATFORM_INDEX}" ]]; then
    echo "[FAIL] Step-02 platform public API is missing."
    exit 1
fi

if [[ -e "${CONTRACT_FILE}" ]]; then
    echo "[FAIL] Step-03 contract already exists."
    echo "Stop and reconcile before continuing."
    exit 1
fi

if [[ -e "${COORDINATION_DIR}" ]]; then
    echo "[FAIL] Step-03 coordination directory already exists."
    echo "Stop and reconcile before continuing."
    exit 1
fi

echo "[PASS] Step-02 contract boundary confirmed."
echo "[PASS] Step-03 target surface does not already exist."

# ------------------------------------------------------------------------------
# 02 — CREATE COORDINATION DIRECTORY
# ------------------------------------------------------------------------------

mkdir -p "${COORDINATION_DIR}"

# ------------------------------------------------------------------------------
# 03 — WORKSPACE OPERATIONAL COORDINATOR CONTRACT
# ------------------------------------------------------------------------------

cat > "${CONTRACT_FILE}" <<'TS'
/**
 * =============================================================================
 * COREI — INSTITUTIONAL ALGORITHMIC TRADING PLATFORM
 * =============================================================================
 *
 * File:
 *   workspace-operational-coordinator.contract.ts
 *
 * Stage:
 *   Stage-25 — Platform Enablement
 *
 * Phase:
 *   Phase-19 — Window & Workspace Platform
 *
 * Step:
 *   Step-03 — Workspace Operational Coordination
 *
 * Purpose:
 *   Defines the provider-independent operational coordination contract for
 *   workspace references known to the Phase-19 platform boundary.
 *
 * Architectural Boundary:
 *   This contract coordinates workspace operational state.
 *
 *   It does not replace:
 *   - existing workspace registries;
 *   - existing workspace runtimes;
 *   - existing workspace loaders;
 *   - existing workspace persistence;
 *   - existing Workbench composition.
 *
 * =============================================================================
 */

import type {
  WorkspaceId,
  WorkspaceLifecycleState,
  WorkspaceReference,
} from "./window-workspace-platform.types";

/* =============================================================================
 * SECTION 01 — COORDINATOR SNAPSHOT
 * =============================================================================
 */

export interface WorkspaceOperationalSnapshot {
  readonly activeWorkspaceId: WorkspaceId | null;
  readonly workspaces: readonly WorkspaceReference[];
}

/* =============================================================================
 * SECTION 02 — COORDINATOR CONTRACT
 * =============================================================================
 */

export interface WorkspaceOperationalCoordinatorContract {
  /**
   * Registers a workspace reference with the Phase-19 coordination boundary.
   *
   * Duplicate workspace identities are rejected.
   */
  registerWorkspace(workspace: WorkspaceReference): void;

  /**
   * Removes a known workspace from the coordination boundary.
   *
   * Removing the active workspace also clears the active workspace identity.
   */
  unregisterWorkspace(workspaceId: WorkspaceId): void;

  /**
   * Returns whether a workspace identity is currently known.
   */
  hasWorkspace(workspaceId: WorkspaceId): boolean;

  /**
   * Returns a workspace reference when known.
   */
  getWorkspace(workspaceId: WorkspaceId): WorkspaceReference | null;

  /**
   * Returns an immutable snapshot of all known workspace references.
   */
  getWorkspaces(): readonly WorkspaceReference[];

  /**
   * Updates the lifecycle state of a known workspace.
   */
  setWorkspaceLifecycleState(
    workspaceId: WorkspaceId,
    lifecycleState: WorkspaceLifecycleState,
  ): void;

  /**
   * Makes a known workspace the active workspace.
   */
  setActiveWorkspace(workspaceId: WorkspaceId): void;

  /**
   * Clears the active workspace identity.
   */
  clearActiveWorkspace(): void;

  /**
   * Returns the active workspace identity when one exists.
   */
  getActiveWorkspaceId(): WorkspaceId | null;

  /**
   * Returns an immutable operational snapshot.
   */
  getSnapshot(): WorkspaceOperationalSnapshot;
}
TS

# ------------------------------------------------------------------------------
# 04 — WORKSPACE OPERATIONAL COORDINATOR IMPLEMENTATION
# ------------------------------------------------------------------------------

cat > "${COORDINATOR_FILE}" <<'TS'
/**
 * =============================================================================
 * COREI — INSTITUTIONAL ALGORITHMIC TRADING PLATFORM
 * =============================================================================
 *
 * File:
 *   workspace-operational-coordinator.ts
 *
 * Stage:
 *   Stage-25 — Platform Enablement
 *
 * Phase:
 *   Phase-19 — Window & Workspace Platform
 *
 * Step:
 *   Step-03 — Workspace Operational Coordination
 *
 * Purpose:
 *   Provides deterministic in-memory operational coordination for workspace
 *   references known to the Phase-19 platform boundary.
 *
 * Ownership:
 *   - Owns Phase-19 coordination state only.
 *   - Does not own the underlying workspace runtime.
 *   - Does not load or render workspaces.
 *   - Does not persist workspace state.
 *   - Does not publish events.
 *   - Does not integrate Dockview.
 *
 * =============================================================================
 */

import type {
  WorkspaceOperationalCoordinatorContract,
  WorkspaceOperationalSnapshot,
} from "../contracts/workspace-operational-coordinator.contract";

import type {
  WorkspaceId,
  WorkspaceLifecycleState,
  WorkspaceReference,
} from "../contracts/window-workspace-platform.types";

/* =============================================================================
 * SECTION 01 — ERROR MESSAGES
 * =============================================================================
 */

const duplicateWorkspaceMessage = (workspaceId: WorkspaceId): string =>
  `Workspace is already registered: ${workspaceId}`;

const unknownWorkspaceMessage = (workspaceId: WorkspaceId): string =>
  `Workspace is not registered: ${workspaceId}`;

/* =============================================================================
 * SECTION 02 — INTERNAL SNAPSHOT HELPERS
 * =============================================================================
 */

const copyWorkspaceReference = (
  workspace: WorkspaceReference,
): WorkspaceReference => ({
  workspaceId: workspace.workspaceId,
  lifecycleState: workspace.lifecycleState,
});

/* =============================================================================
 * SECTION 03 — WORKSPACE OPERATIONAL COORDINATOR
 * =============================================================================
 */

export class WorkspaceOperationalCoordinator
  implements WorkspaceOperationalCoordinatorContract
{
  private readonly workspaces = new Map<WorkspaceId, WorkspaceReference>();

  private activeWorkspaceId: WorkspaceId | null = null;

  /* ===========================================================================
   * REGISTRATION
   * ===========================================================================
   */

  public registerWorkspace(workspace: WorkspaceReference): void {
    if (this.workspaces.has(workspace.workspaceId)) {
      throw new Error(duplicateWorkspaceMessage(workspace.workspaceId));
    }

    this.workspaces.set(
      workspace.workspaceId,
      copyWorkspaceReference(workspace),
    );
  }

  public unregisterWorkspace(workspaceId: WorkspaceId): void {
    this.requireWorkspace(workspaceId);

    this.workspaces.delete(workspaceId);

    if (this.activeWorkspaceId === workspaceId) {
      this.activeWorkspaceId = null;
    }
  }

  /* ===========================================================================
   * LOOKUP
   * ===========================================================================
   */

  public hasWorkspace(workspaceId: WorkspaceId): boolean {
    return this.workspaces.has(workspaceId);
  }

  public getWorkspace(workspaceId: WorkspaceId): WorkspaceReference | null {
    const workspace = this.workspaces.get(workspaceId);

    return workspace === undefined
      ? null
      : copyWorkspaceReference(workspace);
  }

  public getWorkspaces(): readonly WorkspaceReference[] {
    return Array.from(
      this.workspaces.values(),
      copyWorkspaceReference,
    );
  }

  /* ===========================================================================
   * LIFECYCLE COORDINATION
   * ===========================================================================
   */

  public setWorkspaceLifecycleState(
    workspaceId: WorkspaceId,
    lifecycleState: WorkspaceLifecycleState,
  ): void {
    const workspace = this.requireWorkspace(workspaceId);

    this.workspaces.set(workspaceId, {
      workspaceId: workspace.workspaceId,
      lifecycleState,
    });
  }

  /* ===========================================================================
   * ACTIVE WORKSPACE COORDINATION
   * ===========================================================================
   */

  public setActiveWorkspace(workspaceId: WorkspaceId): void {
    this.requireWorkspace(workspaceId);
    this.activeWorkspaceId = workspaceId;
  }

  public clearActiveWorkspace(): void {
    this.activeWorkspaceId = null;
  }

  public getActiveWorkspaceId(): WorkspaceId | null {
    return this.activeWorkspaceId;
  }

  /* ===========================================================================
   * SNAPSHOT
   * ===========================================================================
   */

  public getSnapshot(): WorkspaceOperationalSnapshot {
    return {
      activeWorkspaceId: this.activeWorkspaceId,
      workspaces: this.getWorkspaces(),
    };
  }

  /* ===========================================================================
   * INTERNAL INVARIANT ENFORCEMENT
   * ===========================================================================
   */

  private requireWorkspace(workspaceId: WorkspaceId): WorkspaceReference {
    const workspace = this.workspaces.get(workspaceId);

    if (workspace === undefined) {
      throw new Error(unknownWorkspaceMessage(workspaceId));
    }

    return workspace;
  }
}
TS

1. Step-04 Objective

Establish the authoritative Phase-19 window operational management boundary.

The Window Manager will own:

window registration;
window unregistration;
window lookup;
immutable window snapshots;
window lifecycle-state coordination;
active-window coordination;
workspace ownership association;
reassignment of a window to another registered workspace;
detachment of a window from a workspace;
clearing active-window identity when the active window is removed.

2. Architectural Boundary

The resulting Phase-19 architecture becomes:

Window & Workspace Platform Contract
        │
        ├──────────────────────────────────────┐
        │                                      │
        ▼                                      ▼
Workspace Operational                 Window Management
Coordinator                           Platform
Step-03                               Step-04
        │                                      │
        ├── workspace identity                 ├── window identity
        ├── workspace lifecycle                ├── window lifecycle
        ├── active workspace                   ├── active window
        └── workspace snapshot                 ├── workspace association
                                               └── window snapshot

The important ownership rule is:

WorkspaceOperationalCoordinator
    owns workspace coordination state

WindowManager
    owns window coordination state

WindowManager
    consults workspace coordination
    when establishing workspace ownership

WindowManager
    does NOT become a second workspace registry

This prevents duplicated workspace authority.

3. Step-04 Operational Invariants
INVARIANT-01
Window identity is unique.

INVARIANT-02
Duplicate window registration is rejected.

INVARIANT-03
Unknown window mutation is rejected.

INVARIANT-04
A workspace-associated window may reference only a workspace known to the
WorkspaceOperationalCoordinator.

INVARIANT-05
An active window must already be registered.

INVARIANT-06
Removing the active window clears activeWindowId.

INVARIANT-07
Workspace reassignment requires a registered target workspace.

INVARIANT-08
Window detachment removes workspace association without removing the window.

INVARIANT-09
Returned window references and snapshots are copies of internal records.

INVARIANT-10
Window management remains independent of React, Dockview, Zustand, Shell,
existing windowing implementations, and provider implementations.

│
├── coordination/
│   ├── window-manager.ts                             NEW
│   └── index.ts                                      UPDATE
│
└── index.ts                                          UPDATE

Therefore:

No other source file is authorized.

#!/usr/bin/env bash

# ==============================================================================
# COREI — STAGE-25 / PHASE-19 / STEP-04
# ==============================================================================
#
# Step:
#   Window Management Platform
#

COORDINATION_INDEX="${TARGET}/coordination/index.ts"

echo "WINDOW MANAGEMENT PLATFORM"

# ------------------------------------------------------------------------------

REQUIRED_PRECONDITIONS=(
  "${TARGET}/contracts/window-workspace-platform.types.ts"
  "${TARGET}/contracts/window-workspace-platform.contract.ts"

# ------------------------------------------------------------------------------
# 02 — WINDOW MANAGEMENT CONTRACT
# ------------------------------------------------------------------------------

cat > "${CONTRACT_FILE}" <<'TS'
/**
 * =============================================================================
 * COREI — INSTITUTIONAL ALGORITHMIC TRADING PLATFORM
 * =============================================================================
 *
 * File:
 *   window-management.contract.ts
 *
 * Stage:
 *   Stage-25 — Platform Enablement
 *
 * Phase:
 *   Phase-19 — Window & Workspace Platform
 *
 * Step:
 *   Step-04 — Window Management Platform
 *
 * Purpose:
 *   Defines the provider-independent operational management contract for
 *   window references known to the Phase-19 platform boundary.
 *
 * Architectural Boundary:
 *   This contract coordinates logical platform windows.
 *
 *   It does not define or own:
 *   - browser Window objects;
 *   - DOM rendering;
 *   - Dockview panels;
 *   - layout geometry;
 *   - floating-window geometry;
 *   - persistence;
 *   - restoration;
 *   - multi-monitor placement.
 *
 * =============================================================================
 */

import type {
  WindowId,
  WindowLifecycleState,
  WindowReference,
  WorkspaceId,
} from "./window-workspace-platform.types";

/* =============================================================================
 * SECTION 01 — WINDOW MANAGEMENT SNAPSHOT
 * =============================================================================
 */

export interface WindowManagementSnapshot {
  readonly activeWindowId: WindowId | null;
  readonly windows: readonly WindowReference[];
}

/* =============================================================================
 * SECTION 02 — WINDOW MANAGEMENT CONTRACT
 * =============================================================================
 */

export interface WindowManagementContract {
  /**
   * Registers a logical window with the Phase-19 platform boundary.
   *
   * Duplicate window identities are rejected.
   *
   * When workspaceId is non-null, that workspace must already be known to the
   * workspace coordination boundary.
   */
  registerWindow(window: WindowReference): void;

  /**
   * Removes a known logical window.
   *
   * Removing the active window also clears the active window identity.
   */
  unregisterWindow(windowId: WindowId): void;

  /**
   * Returns whether a window identity is currently known.
   */
  hasWindow(windowId: WindowId): boolean;

  /**
   * Returns a window reference when known.
   */
  getWindow(windowId: WindowId): WindowReference | null;

  /**
   * Returns an immutable snapshot of all known window references.
   */
  getWindows(): readonly WindowReference[];

  /**
   * Updates the lifecycle state of a known window.
   */
  setWindowLifecycleState(
    windowId: WindowId,
    lifecycleState: WindowLifecycleState,
  ): void;

  /**
   * Associates a known window with a known workspace.
   */
  assignWindowToWorkspace(
    windowId: WindowId,
    workspaceId: WorkspaceId,
  ): void;

  /**
   * Removes workspace association from a known window.
   */
  detachWindowFromWorkspace(windowId: WindowId): void;

  /**
   * Makes a known window the active window.
   */
  setActiveWindow(windowId: WindowId): void;

  /**
   * Clears the active window identity.
   */
  clearActiveWindow(): void;

  /**
   * Returns the active window identity when one exists.
   */
  getActiveWindowId(): WindowId | null;

  /**
   * Returns an immutable operational snapshot.
   */
  getSnapshot(): WindowManagementSnapshot;
}
TS

# ------------------------------------------------------------------------------
# 03 — WINDOW MANAGER IMPLEMENTATION
# ------------------------------------------------------------------------------

cat > "${WINDOW_MANAGER_FILE}" <<'TS'
/**
 * =============================================================================
 * COREI — INSTITUTIONAL ALGORITHMIC TRADING PLATFORM
 * =============================================================================
 *
 * File:
 *   window-manager.ts
 *
 * Stage:
 *   Stage-25 — Platform Enablement
 *
 * Phase:
 *   Phase-19 — Window & Workspace Platform
 *
 * Step:
 *   Step-04 — Window Management Platform
 *
 * Purpose:
 *   Provides deterministic in-memory operational management for logical window
 *   references known to the Phase-19 platform boundary.
 *
 * Ownership:
 *   - Owns Phase-19 logical window coordination state.
 *   - Consults workspace coordination for workspace identity validity.
 *   - Does not own the workspace registry.
 *   - Does not create browser windows.
 *   - Does not render UI.
 *   - Does not integrate Dockview.
 *   - Does not persist window state.
 *   - Does not publish events.
 *
 * =============================================================================
 */

import type {
  WindowManagementContract,
  WindowManagementSnapshot,
} from "../contracts/window-management.contract";

import type {
  WorkspaceOperationalCoordinatorContract,
} from "../contracts/workspace-operational-coordinator.contract";

import type {
  WindowId,
  WindowLifecycleState,
  WindowReference,
  WorkspaceId,
} from "../contracts/window-workspace-platform.types";

/* =============================================================================
 * SECTION 01 — ERROR MESSAGES
 * =============================================================================
 */

const duplicateWindowMessage = (windowId: WindowId): string =>
  `Window is already registered: ${windowId}`;

const unknownWindowMessage = (windowId: WindowId): string =>
  `Window is not registered: ${windowId}`;

const unknownWorkspaceMessage = (workspaceId: WorkspaceId): string =>
  `Workspace is not registered: ${workspaceId}`;

/* =============================================================================
 * SECTION 02 — INTERNAL SNAPSHOT HELPERS
 * =============================================================================
 */

const copyWindowReference = (
  window: WindowReference,
): WindowReference => ({
  windowId: window.windowId,
  workspaceId: window.workspaceId,
  lifecycleState: window.lifecycleState,
});

/* =============================================================================
 * SECTION 03 — WINDOW MANAGER
 * =============================================================================
 */

export class WindowManager implements WindowManagementContract {
  private readonly windows = new Map<WindowId, WindowReference>();

  private activeWindowId: WindowId | null = null;

  public constructor(
    private readonly workspaceCoordinator:
      WorkspaceOperationalCoordinatorContract,
  ) {}

  /* ===========================================================================
   * REGISTRATION
   * ===========================================================================
   */

  public registerWindow(window: WindowReference): void {
    if (this.windows.has(window.windowId)) {
      throw new Error(duplicateWindowMessage(window.windowId));
    }

    if (window.workspaceId !== null) {
      this.requireWorkspace(window.workspaceId);
    }

    this.windows.set(
      window.windowId,
      copyWindowReference(window),
    );
  }

  public unregisterWindow(windowId: WindowId): void {
    this.requireWindow(windowId);

    this.windows.delete(windowId);

    if (this.activeWindowId === windowId) {
      this.activeWindowId = null;
    }
  }

  /* ===========================================================================
   * LOOKUP
   * ===========================================================================
   */

  public hasWindow(windowId: WindowId): boolean {
    return this.windows.has(windowId);
  }

  public getWindow(windowId: WindowId): WindowReference | null {
    const window = this.windows.get(windowId);

    return window === undefined
      ? null
      : copyWindowReference(window);
  }

  public getWindows(): readonly WindowReference[] {
    return Array.from(
      this.windows.values(),
      copyWindowReference,
    );
  }

  /* ===========================================================================
   * LIFECYCLE COORDINATION
   * ===========================================================================
   */

  public setWindowLifecycleState(
    windowId: WindowId,
    lifecycleState: WindowLifecycleState,
  ): void {
    const window = this.requireWindow(windowId);

    this.windows.set(windowId, {
      windowId: window.windowId,
      workspaceId: window.workspaceId,
      lifecycleState,
    });
  }

  /* ===========================================================================
   * WORKSPACE ASSOCIATION
   * ===========================================================================
   */

  public assignWindowToWorkspace(
    windowId: WindowId,
    workspaceId: WorkspaceId,
  ): void {
    const window = this.requireWindow(windowId);

    this.requireWorkspace(workspaceId);

    this.windows.set(windowId, {
      windowId: window.windowId,
      workspaceId,
      lifecycleState: window.lifecycleState,
    });
  }

  public detachWindowFromWorkspace(windowId: WindowId): void {
    const window = this.requireWindow(windowId);

    this.windows.set(windowId, {
      windowId: window.windowId,
      workspaceId: null,
      lifecycleState: window.lifecycleState,
    });
  }

  /* ===========================================================================
   * ACTIVE WINDOW COORDINATION
   * ===========================================================================
   */

  public setActiveWindow(windowId: WindowId): void {
    this.requireWindow(windowId);
    this.activeWindowId = windowId;
  }

  public clearActiveWindow(): void {
    this.activeWindowId = null;
  }

  public getActiveWindowId(): WindowId | null {
    return this.activeWindowId;
  }

  /* ===========================================================================
   * SNAPSHOT
   * ===========================================================================
   */

  public getSnapshot(): WindowManagementSnapshot {
    return {
      activeWindowId: this.activeWindowId,
      windows: this.getWindows(),
    };
  }

  /* ===========================================================================
   * INTERNAL INVARIANT ENFORCEMENT
   * ===========================================================================
   */

  private requireWindow(windowId: WindowId): WindowReference {
    const window = this.windows.get(windowId);

    if (window === undefined) {
      throw new Error(unknownWindowMessage(windowId));
    }

    return window;
  }

  private requireWorkspace(workspaceId: WorkspaceId): void {
    if (!this.workspaceCoordinator.hasWorkspace(workspaceId)) {
      throw new Error(unknownWorkspaceMessage(workspaceId));
    }
  }
}
TS

cat >> "${CONTRACTS_INDEX}" <<'TS'

} from "./window-management.contract";

1. Step-05 Objective

Step-05 establishes the controlled integration bridge between the new Phase-19 logical Window & Workspace Platform and the existing Dockview foundation boundary discovered and protected during Step-01.

The critical rule is:

Step-05 must consume the existing Dockview foundation. It must not install Dockview again, rebuild the Dockview provider, replace the existing workspace foundation, or create a parallel docking engine.

The integration direction is:

Phase-19 Logical Platform
        │
        ├── WorkspaceOperationalCoordinator
        └── WindowManager
                │
                ▼
      Dockview Runtime Integration
              STEP-05
                │
                ▼
 Existing Foundation Workspace Boundary
      src/foundation/workspace
                │
                ▼
             Dockview

Step-05 is therefore an adapter/integration step, not a new docking implementation.

2. Browser Visibility Changes

changing the Shell layout;
adding visible tabs;
adding visible split panes;
adding visible docking controls;
changing panel positions;
adding floating windows;
changing workspace appearance;
changing CSS;
changing visual hierarchy.

Current certified institutional Shell remains visible.

AFTER STEP-05
The same certified institutional Shell remains visible.

NONE INTENTIONALLY INTRODUCED.

BEFORE
Phase-19 logical platform exists independently.

AFTER
Phase-19 gains a typed integration boundary capable of controlling an
existing Dockview runtime when a runtime binding is attached.

So the Step-05 browser validation is:

The application must still load successfully, the existing Shell must remain visually intact, and no unintended visual regression should appear.

Actual visible docking/layout behavior belongs to subsequent authorized steps.

3. Step-05 Architectural Boundary

Step-05 will establish:

a provider-neutral Dockview runtime integration contract;
a deterministic runtime integration coordinator;
an adapter interface through which the existing Dockview foundation can later bind;
runtime attachment/detachment;
readiness state;
logical workspace/window synchronization commands;
immutable integration snapshots.

Step-05 will not implement:

Dockview layout serialization;
split/tab/group operations;
floating windows;
multi-window browser behavior;
multi-monitor behavior;
persistence;
restoration;
Shell mounting changes;
visual workspace switching.

WindowWorkspacePlatform
        │

Existing Foundation
    = Dockview technology authority

At Step-05, we establish the seam.

We do not modify:

src/foundation/workspace/

because it remains a protected boundary.

INVARIANT-06

already owned by WindowManager.

INVARIANT-10
No visual Shell modification is authorized.

Run this entire block exactly once:

cd ~/corei/ui/corei-os

cat > /tmp/stage25-phase19-step05-implement.sh <<'EOF'
#!/usr/bin/env bash

# ==============================================================================
# COREI — STAGE-25 / PHASE-19 / STEP-05
# ==============================================================================
#
# Step:
#   Dockview Runtime Integration
#
# Purpose:
#   Establish the controlled provider-facing integration seam between the
#   Phase-19 logical Window & Workspace Platform and the existing Dockview
#   workspace foundation.
#
# Scope:
#   - Dockview runtime adapter contract
#   - Dockview runtime integration contract
#   - Deterministic runtime attachment / detachment
#   - Workspace synchronization
#   - Window synchronization
#   - Public API exposure
#
# Explicitly Not Implemented:
#   - New Dockview provider
#   - New Dockview installation
#   - Layout runtime
#   - Split / tab / group operations
#   - Floating windows
#   - Browser multi-window runtime
#   - Multi-monitor runtime
#   - Persistence
#   - Restoration
#   - Recovery
#   - Shell modification
#   - Visual UI modification
#
# Browser Visibility:
#   No intentional visual change is authorized by Step-05.
#
# ==============================================================================

set -euo pipefail

ROOT="$(pwd)"
TARGET="${ROOT}/src/window-workspace-platform"

CONTRACT_FILE="${TARGET}/contracts/dockview-runtime-integration.contract.ts"
INTEGRATION_DIR="${TARGET}/integration"
INTEGRATION_FILE="${INTEGRATION_DIR}/dockview-runtime-integration.ts"
INTEGRATION_INDEX="${INTEGRATION_DIR}/index.ts"

CONTRACTS_INDEX="${TARGET}/contracts/index.ts"
PLATFORM_INDEX="${TARGET}/index.ts"

echo "============================================================================"
echo "COREI — STAGE-25 → PHASE-19 → STEP-05"
echo "DOCKVIEW RUNTIME INTEGRATION"
echo "============================================================================"
echo
echo "ROOT=${ROOT}"
echo

# ------------------------------------------------------------------------------
# 01 — PRECONDITION VALIDATION
# ------------------------------------------------------------------------------

REQUIRED_PRECONDITIONS=(
  "${TARGET}/contracts/window-workspace-platform.types.ts"
  "${TARGET}/contracts/workspace-operational-coordinator.contract.ts"
  "${TARGET}/contracts/window-management.contract.ts"
  "${TARGET}/coordination/workspace-operational-coordinator.ts"
  "${TARGET}/coordination/window-manager.ts"
  "${CONTRACTS_INDEX}"
  "${PLATFORM_INDEX}"
)

for file in "${REQUIRED_PRECONDITIONS[@]}"; do
  if [[ ! -s "${file}" ]]; then
    echo "[FAIL] Required prerequisite missing or empty: ${file}"
    exit 1
  fi
done

if [[ ! -d "${ROOT}/src/foundation/workspace" ]]; then
  echo "[FAIL] Existing foundation workspace boundary is missing."
  exit 1
fi

if [[ -e "${CONTRACT_FILE}" ]]; then
  echo "[FAIL] Step-05 contract already exists."
  echo "Stop and reconcile before continuing."
  exit 1
fi

if [[ -e "${INTEGRATION_DIR}" ]]; then
  echo "[FAIL] Step-05 integration directory already exists."
  echo "Stop and reconcile before continuing."
  exit 1
fi

echo "[PASS] Step-03 workspace coordination confirmed."
echo "[PASS] Step-04 window management confirmed."
echo "[PASS] Existing foundation workspace boundary confirmed."
echo "[PASS] Step-05 target surface does not already exist."

# ------------------------------------------------------------------------------
# 02 — CREATE INTEGRATION DIRECTORY
# ------------------------------------------------------------------------------

mkdir -p "${INTEGRATION_DIR}"

# ------------------------------------------------------------------------------
# 03 — DOCKVIEW RUNTIME INTEGRATION CONTRACT
# ------------------------------------------------------------------------------

cat > "${CONTRACT_FILE}" <<'TS'
/**
 * =============================================================================
 * COREI — INSTITUTIONAL ALGORITHMIC TRADING PLATFORM
 * =============================================================================
 *
 * File:
 *   dockview-runtime-integration.contract.ts
 *
 * Stage:
 *   Stage-25 — Platform Enablement
 *
 * Phase:
 *   Phase-19 — Window & Workspace Platform
 *
 * Step:
 *   Step-05 — Dockview Runtime Integration
 *
 * Purpose:
 *   Defines the controlled provider-facing integration seam between the
 *   Phase-19 logical Window & Workspace Platform and an existing Dockview
 *   runtime implementation.
 *
 * Architectural Boundary:
 *   - The Phase-19 core does not directly import Dockview.
 *   - The adapter represents the existing Dockview runtime boundary.
 *   - The integration layer coordinates logical state with that adapter.
 *   - The integration layer does not own workspace or window registries.
 *
 * Browser Visibility:
 *   Step-05 introduces no intentional visual change.
 *
 * =============================================================================
 */

import type {
  WindowId,
  WindowReference,
  WorkspaceId,
  WorkspaceReference,
} from "./window-workspace-platform.types";

/* =============================================================================
 * SECTION 01 — RUNTIME ADAPTER CONTRACT
 * =============================================================================
 */

export interface DockviewRuntimeAdapterContract {
  /**
   * Returns whether the underlying Dockview runtime is ready to receive
   * synchronization commands.
   */
  isReady(): boolean;

  /**
   * Synchronizes a known logical workspace with the existing Dockview runtime.
   */
  synchronizeWorkspace(workspace: WorkspaceReference): void;

  /**
   * Synchronizes a known logical window with the existing Dockview runtime.
   */
  synchronizeWindow(window: WindowReference): void;

  /**
   * Removes a workspace representation from the existing Dockview runtime.
   */
  removeWorkspace(workspaceId: WorkspaceId): void;

  /**
   * Removes a window representation from the existing Dockview runtime.
   */
  removeWindow(windowId: WindowId): void;
}

/* =============================================================================
 * SECTION 02 — INTEGRATION SNAPSHOT
 * =============================================================================
 */

export interface DockviewRuntimeIntegrationSnapshot {
  readonly attached: boolean;
  readonly ready: boolean;
}

/* =============================================================================
 * SECTION 03 — INTEGRATION CONTRACT
 * =============================================================================
 */

export interface DockviewRuntimeIntegrationContract {
  /**
   * Attaches the existing Dockview runtime adapter.
   *
   * Duplicate attachment is rejected.
   */
  attachRuntime(adapter: DockviewRuntimeAdapterContract): void;

  /**
   * Detaches the currently attached runtime adapter.
   */
  detachRuntime(): void;

  /**
   * Returns whether a runtime adapter is attached.
   */
  isAttached(): boolean;

  /**
   * Returns whether the attached runtime is ready.
   */
  isReady(): boolean;

  /**
   * Synchronizes a known workspace through the attached runtime adapter.
   */
  synchronizeWorkspace(workspaceId: WorkspaceId): void;

  /**
   * Synchronizes a known window through the attached runtime adapter.
   */
  synchronizeWindow(windowId: WindowId): void;

  /**
   * Removes a known workspace representation from the attached runtime.
   */
  removeWorkspace(workspaceId: WorkspaceId): void;

  /**
   * Removes a known window representation from the attached runtime.
   */
  removeWindow(windowId: WindowId): void;

  /**
   * Returns the current immutable integration snapshot.
   */
  getSnapshot(): DockviewRuntimeIntegrationSnapshot;
}
TS

# ------------------------------------------------------------------------------
# 04 — DOCKVIEW RUNTIME INTEGRATION IMPLEMENTATION
# ------------------------------------------------------------------------------

cat > "${INTEGRATION_FILE}" <<'TS'
/**
 * =============================================================================
 * COREI — INSTITUTIONAL ALGORITHMIC TRADING PLATFORM
 * =============================================================================
 *
 * File:
 *   dockview-runtime-integration.ts
 *
 * Stage:
 *   Stage-25 — Platform Enablement
 *
 * Phase:
 *   Phase-19 — Window & Workspace Platform
 *
 * Step:
 *   Step-05 — Dockview Runtime Integration
 *
 * Purpose:
 *   Coordinates the Phase-19 logical workspace and window authorities with an
 *   attached adapter representing the existing Dockview runtime boundary.
 *
 * Ownership:
 *   - Owns runtime attachment state only.
 *   - Does not own workspace state.
 *   - Does not own window state.
 *   - Does not directly import Dockview.
 *   - Does not render UI.
 *   - Does not own layout geometry.
 *   - Does not persist runtime state.
 *
 * Browser Visibility:
 *   No intentional visual change is introduced by this implementation.
 *
 * =============================================================================
 */

import type {
  DockviewRuntimeAdapterContract,
  DockviewRuntimeIntegrationContract,
  DockviewRuntimeIntegrationSnapshot,
} from "../contracts/dockview-runtime-integration.contract";

import type {
  WindowManagementContract,
} from "../contracts/window-management.contract";

import type {
  WorkspaceOperationalCoordinatorContract,
} from "../contracts/workspace-operational-coordinator.contract";

import type {
  WindowId,
  WorkspaceId,
} from "../contracts/window-workspace-platform.types";

/* =============================================================================
 * SECTION 01 — ERROR MESSAGES
 * =============================================================================
 */

const runtimeAlreadyAttachedMessage =
  "Dockview runtime adapter is already attached.";

const runtimeNotAttachedMessage =
  "Dockview runtime adapter is not attached.";

const runtimeNotReadyMessage =
  "Dockview runtime adapter is not ready.";

const unknownWorkspaceMessage = (workspaceId: WorkspaceId): string =>
  `Workspace is not registered: ${workspaceId}`;

const unknownWindowMessage = (windowId: WindowId): string =>
  `Window is not registered: ${windowId}`;

/* =============================================================================
 * SECTION 02 — DOCKVIEW RUNTIME INTEGRATION
 * =============================================================================
 */

export class DockviewRuntimeIntegration
  implements DockviewRuntimeIntegrationContract
{
  private runtimeAdapter: DockviewRuntimeAdapterContract | null = null;

  public constructor(
    private readonly workspaceCoordinator:
      WorkspaceOperationalCoordinatorContract,
    private readonly windowManager:
      WindowManagementContract,
  ) {}

  /* ===========================================================================
   * RUNTIME ATTACHMENT
   * ===========================================================================
   */

  public attachRuntime(adapter: DockviewRuntimeAdapterContract): void {
    if (this.runtimeAdapter !== null) {
      throw new Error(runtimeAlreadyAttachedMessage);
    }

    this.runtimeAdapter = adapter;
  }

  public detachRuntime(): void {
    this.runtimeAdapter = null;
  }

  public isAttached(): boolean {
    return this.runtimeAdapter !== null;
  }

  public isReady(): boolean {
    return this.runtimeAdapter?.isReady() ?? false;
  }

  /* ===========================================================================
   * WORKSPACE SYNCHRONIZATION
   * ===========================================================================
   */

  public synchronizeWorkspace(workspaceId: WorkspaceId): void {
    const runtime = this.requireReadyRuntime();
    const workspace = this.workspaceCoordinator.getWorkspace(workspaceId);

    if (workspace === null) {
      throw new Error(unknownWorkspaceMessage(workspaceId));
    }

    runtime.synchronizeWorkspace(workspace);
  }

  public removeWorkspace(workspaceId: WorkspaceId): void {
    const runtime = this.requireReadyRuntime();

    if (!this.workspaceCoordinator.hasWorkspace(workspaceId)) {
      throw new Error(unknownWorkspaceMessage(workspaceId));
    }

    runtime.removeWorkspace(workspaceId);
  }

  /* ===========================================================================
   * WINDOW SYNCHRONIZATION
   * ===========================================================================
   */

  public synchronizeWindow(windowId: WindowId): void {
    const runtime = this.requireReadyRuntime();
    const window = this.windowManager.getWindow(windowId);

    if (window === null) {
      throw new Error(unknownWindowMessage(windowId));
    }

    runtime.synchronizeWindow(window);
  }

  public removeWindow(windowId: WindowId): void {
    const runtime = this.requireReadyRuntime();

    if (!this.windowManager.hasWindow(windowId)) {
      throw new Error(unknownWindowMessage(windowId));
    }

    runtime.removeWindow(windowId);
  }

  /* ===========================================================================
   * SNAPSHOT
   * ===========================================================================
   */

  public getSnapshot(): DockviewRuntimeIntegrationSnapshot {
    return {
      attached: this.isAttached(),
      ready: this.isReady(),
    };
  }

  /* ===========================================================================
   * INTERNAL INVARIANT ENFORCEMENT
   * ===========================================================================
   */

  private requireReadyRuntime(): DockviewRuntimeAdapterContract {
    if (this.runtimeAdapter === null) {
      throw new Error(runtimeNotAttachedMessage);
    }

    if (!this.runtimeAdapter.isReady()) {
      throw new Error(runtimeNotReadyMessage);
    }

    return this.runtimeAdapter;
  }
}
TS

4. Step-06 Operational Invariants

Layout identity is unique.

INVARIANT-02

INVARIANT-03

INVARIANT-04
Unknown layout mutation is rejected.

An active layout must already be registered.

INVARIANT-06
The active layout must belong to the workspace for which it is activated.

INVARIANT-07
Each workspace has at most one active layout.

INVARIANT-08
Removing an active layout clears that workspace's active layout identity.

INVARIANT-09
Returned layout references and snapshots are copies of internal records.

INVARIANT-10
Layout Runtime remains provider-independent and does not directly import
Dockview, React, Zustand, browser APIs, or Shell implementation surfaces.

#!/usr/bin/env bash

# ==============================================================================
# COREI — STAGE-25 / PHASE-19 / STEP-06
# ==============================================================================
#
# Step:
#   Layout Runtime
#
# Purpose:
#   Establish deterministic logical layout state management for the Phase-19
#   Window & Workspace Platform.
#
# Scope:
#   - Layout identity
#   - Layout lifecycle state
#   - Layout registration / removal
#   - Workspace ownership validation
#   - Active layout coordination per workspace
#   - Immutable layout snapshots
#   - Public API exposure
#
# Explicitly Not Implemented:
#   - Dockview layout serialization
#   - Dockview toJSON / fromJSON
#   - Split operations
#   - Tab operations
#   - Group operations
#   - Panel movement
#   - Floating windows
#   - Browser multi-window runtime
#   - Multi-monitor runtime
#   - Persistence
#   - Restoration
#   - Recovery
#   - Shell integration
#   - Visual UI modification
#
# Browser Visibility:
#   No intentional visual change is authorized by Step-06.
#
# ==============================================================================

set -euo pipefail

ROOT="$(pwd)"
TARGET="${ROOT}/src/window-workspace-platform"

TYPES_FILE="${TARGET}/contracts/window-workspace-platform.types.ts"
CONTRACT_FILE="${TARGET}/contracts/layout-runtime.contract.ts"
