    exit 1
  }

if [[ -e "${CONTRACT}" ]]; then
  echo "[FAIL] Step-16 contract already exists."
  exit 1
fi

if [[ -e "${LIFECYCLE_DIR}" ]]; then
  echo "[FAIL] Step-16 lifecycle directory already exists."
  echo "Stop and reconcile repository reality before continuing."
  exit 1
fi

echo "[PASS] Step-15 Shell ↔ Workbench ↔ Workspace Runtime Integration confirmed."
echo "[PASS] Step-16 target surface does not already exist."

# ------------------------------------------------------------------------------
# 02 — CREATE STEP-16 DIRECTORY
# ------------------------------------------------------------------------------

mkdir -p "${LIFECYCLE_DIR}"

# ------------------------------------------------------------------------------
# 03 — EVENTS / STATE / LIFECYCLE CONTRACT
# ------------------------------------------------------------------------------

cat > "${CONTRACT}" <<'TS'
/**
 * =============================================================================
 * COREI — INSTITUTIONAL ALGORITHMIC TRADING PLATFORM
 * =============================================================================
 *
 * Stage:
 *   Stage-25 — Platform Enablement
 *
 * Phase:
 *   Phase-19 — Window & Workspace Platform
 *
 * Step:
 *   Step-16 — Workspace Events, State & Lifecycle Coordination
 *
 * File:
 *   workspace-events-state-lifecycle.contract.ts
 *
 * Purpose:
 *   Defines the provider-independent coordination boundary for deterministic
 *   Phase-19 workspace, window, layout, persistence, and recovery lifecycle
 *   events and their corresponding projected state.
 *
 * Architectural Ownership:
 *   Phase-19 owns Window & Workspace lifecycle semantics.
 *   The existing COREI Event Platform owns event infrastructure.
 *   The existing COREI State Platform owns state infrastructure.
 *
 * Critical Boundary:
 *   This contract does not create an event bus, global store, provider,
 *   diagnostics platform, telemetry system, or UI state system.
 *
 * Browser Visibility:
 *   Step-16 introduces no intentional visual change.
 *
 * =============================================================================
 */

import type {
  LayoutId,
  WindowId,
  WorkspaceId,
} from "./window-workspace-platform.types";

/* =============================================================================
 * SECTION 01 — EVENT TYPE SURFACE
 * =============================================================================
 */

export type WorkspacePlatformLifecycleEventType =
  | "workspace.opened"
  | "workspace.activated"
  | "workspace.deactivated"
  | "workspace.closed"
  | "window.opened"
  | "window.focused"
  | "window.closed"
  | "layout.changed"
  | "layout.restored"
  | "workspace.persistence.completed"
  | "workspace.recovery.started"
  | "workspace.recovery.completed"
  | "workspace.recovery.failed";

/* =============================================================================
 * SECTION 02 — EVENT IDENTITY / ENVELOPE
 * =============================================================================
 */

export type WorkspacePlatformLifecycleEventSequence =
  number;

export interface WorkspacePlatformLifecycleEvent {
  readonly type:
    WorkspacePlatformLifecycleEventType;

  readonly sequence:
    WorkspacePlatformLifecycleEventSequence;

  readonly workspaceId:
    WorkspaceId | null;

  readonly windowId:
    WindowId | null;

  readonly layoutId:
    LayoutId | null;
}

/* =============================================================================
 * SECTION 03 — PROJECTED PHASE-19 STATE
 * =============================================================================
 */

export interface WorkspacePlatformLifecycleState {
  readonly sequence:
    WorkspacePlatformLifecycleEventSequence;

  readonly activeWorkspaceId:
    WorkspaceId | null;

  readonly activeWindowId:
    WindowId | null;

  readonly activeLayoutId:
    LayoutId | null;

  readonly persistenceCompleted:
    boolean;

  readonly recoveryInProgress:
    boolean;

  readonly recoveryFailed:
    boolean;

  readonly lastEventType:
    WorkspacePlatformLifecycleEventType | null;
}

/* =============================================================================
 * SECTION 04 — EXISTING EVENT PLATFORM ADAPTER PORT
 * =============================================================================
 */

export interface WorkspacePlatformEventPublicationPort {
  publish(
    event:
      Readonly<WorkspacePlatformLifecycleEvent>,
  ): void;
}

/* =============================================================================
 * SECTION 05 — EXISTING STATE PLATFORM ADAPTER PORT
 * =============================================================================
 */

export interface WorkspacePlatformStatePublicationPort {
  publish(
    state:
      Readonly<WorkspacePlatformLifecycleState>,
  ): void;
}

/* =============================================================================
 * SECTION 06 — COORDINATION SNAPSHOT
 * =============================================================================
 */

export interface WorkspaceEventsStateLifecycleSnapshot {
  readonly state:
    Readonly<WorkspacePlatformLifecycleState>;

  readonly events:
    readonly Readonly<WorkspacePlatformLifecycleEvent>[];
}

/* =============================================================================
 * SECTION 07 — COORDINATION CONTRACT
 * =============================================================================
 */

export interface WorkspaceEventsStateLifecycleContract {
  coordinate(
    type:
      WorkspacePlatformLifecycleEventType,

    context?: {
      readonly workspaceId?:
        WorkspaceId | null;

      readonly windowId?:
        WindowId | null;

      readonly layoutId?:
        LayoutId | null;
    },
  ): Readonly<WorkspacePlatformLifecycleEvent>;

  getState():
    Readonly<WorkspacePlatformLifecycleState>;

  getEvents():
    readonly Readonly<WorkspacePlatformLifecycleEvent>[];

  getSnapshot():
    Readonly<WorkspaceEventsStateLifecycleSnapshot>;
}
TS

# ------------------------------------------------------------------------------
# 04 — EVENTS / STATE / LIFECYCLE COORDINATOR
# ------------------------------------------------------------------------------

cat > "${COORDINATOR}" <<'TS'
/**
 * =============================================================================
 * COREI — INSTITUTIONAL ALGORITHMIC TRADING PLATFORM
 * =============================================================================
 *
 * Stage:
 *   Stage-25 — Platform Enablement
 *
 * Phase:
 *   Phase-19 — Window & Workspace Platform
 *
 * Step:
 *   Step-16 — Workspace Events, State & Lifecycle Coordination
 *
 * File:
 *   workspace-events-state-lifecycle-coordinator.ts
 *
 * Purpose:
 *   Coordinates deterministic Phase-19 lifecycle events and state projection,
 *   then delegates publication through injected Event Platform and State
 *   Platform adapter ports.
 *
 * Architectural Boundary:
 *   This coordinator owns Phase-19 coordination semantics only.
 *
 *   It does not implement:
 *   - an event bus;
 *   - a global state store;
 *   - Event Platform infrastructure;
 *   - State Platform infrastructure;
 *   - diagnostics;
 *   - telemetry;
 *   - UI state.
 *
 * =============================================================================
 */

import type {
  WorkspaceEventsStateLifecycleContract,
  WorkspaceEventsStateLifecycleSnapshot,
  WorkspacePlatformEventPublicationPort,
  WorkspacePlatformLifecycleEvent,
  WorkspacePlatformLifecycleEventType,
  WorkspacePlatformLifecycleState,
  WorkspacePlatformStatePublicationPort,
} from "../contracts/workspace-events-state-lifecycle.contract";

/* =============================================================================
 * SECTION 01 — INITIAL STATE
 * =============================================================================
 */

const INITIAL_STATE:
  WorkspacePlatformLifecycleState = {
    sequence: 0,
    activeWorkspaceId: null,
    activeWindowId: null,
    activeLayoutId: null,
    persistenceCompleted: false,
    recoveryInProgress: false,
    recoveryFailed: false,
    lastEventType: null,
  };

/* =============================================================================
 * SECTION 02 — COORDINATOR
 * =============================================================================
 */

export class WorkspaceEventsStateLifecycleCoordinator
  implements WorkspaceEventsStateLifecycleContract
{
  private sequence = 0;

  private state:
    WorkspacePlatformLifecycleState = {
      ...INITIAL_STATE,
    };

  private readonly events:
    WorkspacePlatformLifecycleEvent[] = [];

  public constructor(
    private readonly eventPublicationPort:
      WorkspacePlatformEventPublicationPort,

    private readonly statePublicationPort:
      WorkspacePlatformStatePublicationPort,
  ) {}

  /* ===========================================================================
   * COORDINATION
   * ===========================================================================
   */

  public coordinate(
    type:
      WorkspacePlatformLifecycleEventType,

    context: {
      readonly workspaceId?:
        WorkspacePlatformLifecycleEvent["workspaceId"];

      readonly windowId?:
        WorkspacePlatformLifecycleEvent["windowId"];

      readonly layoutId?:
        WorkspacePlatformLifecycleEvent["layoutId"];
    } = {},
  ): Readonly<WorkspacePlatformLifecycleEvent> {
    this.sequence += 1;

    const event:
      WorkspacePlatformLifecycleEvent = {
        type,
        sequence: this.sequence,
        workspaceId:
          context.workspaceId ?? null,
        windowId:
          context.windowId ?? null,
        layoutId:
          context.layoutId ?? null,
      };

    this.state =
      this.projectState(
        this.state,
        event,
      );

    this.events.push(event);

    this.eventPublicationPort.publish({
      ...event,
    });

    this.statePublicationPort.publish({
      ...this.state,
    });

    return {
      ...event,
    };
  }

  /* ===========================================================================
   * STATE PROJECTION
   * ===========================================================================
   */

  private projectState(
    current:
      Readonly<WorkspacePlatformLifecycleState>,

    event:
      Readonly<WorkspacePlatformLifecycleEvent>,
  ): WorkspacePlatformLifecycleState {
    const next:
      WorkspacePlatformLifecycleState = {
        ...current,
        sequence: event.sequence,
        lastEventType: event.type,
      };

    switch (event.type) {
      case "workspace.opened":
        return next;

      case "workspace.activated":
        return {
          ...next,
          activeWorkspaceId:
            event.workspaceId,
        };

      case "workspace.deactivated":
      case "workspace.closed":
        return {
          ...next,
          activeWorkspaceId:
            current.activeWorkspaceId ===
            event.workspaceId
              ? null
              : current.activeWorkspaceId,
        };

      case "window.opened":
        return next;

      case "window.focused":
        return {
          ...next,
          activeWindowId:
            event.windowId,
        };

      case "window.closed":
        return {
          ...next,
          activeWindowId:
            current.activeWindowId ===
            event.windowId
              ? null
              : current.activeWindowId,
        };

      case "layout.changed":
      case "layout.restored":
        return {
          ...next,
          activeLayoutId:
            event.layoutId,
        };

      case "workspace.persistence.completed":
        return {
          ...next,
          persistenceCompleted:
            true,
        };

      case "workspace.recovery.started":
        return {
          ...next,
          recoveryInProgress:
            true,
          recoveryFailed:
            false,
        };

      case "workspace.recovery.completed":
        return {
          ...next,
          recoveryInProgress:
            false,
          recoveryFailed:
            false,
        };

      case "workspace.recovery.failed":
        return {
          ...next,
          recoveryInProgress:
            false,
          recoveryFailed:
            true,
        };
    }
  }

  /* ===========================================================================
   * READ SURFACE
   * ===========================================================================
   */

  public getState():
    Readonly<WorkspacePlatformLifecycleState> {
    return {
      ...this.state,
    };
  }

  public getEvents():
    readonly Readonly<WorkspacePlatformLifecycleEvent>[] {
    return this.events.map(
      (event) => ({
        ...event,
      }),
    );
  }

  public getSnapshot():
    Readonly<WorkspaceEventsStateLifecycleSnapshot> {
    return {
      state:
        this.getState(),

      events:
        this.getEvents(),
    };
  }
}
TS

# 05 — LIFECYCLE PUBLIC API
# ------------------------------------------------------------------------------

cat > "${LIFECYCLE_INDEX}" <<'TS'

cat > /tmp/stage25-phase19-step16-validate.sh <<'EOF'
#!/usr/bin/env bash

set -euo pipefail

PLATFORM="src/window-workspace-platform"

CONTRACT="${PLATFORM}/contracts/workspace-events-state-lifecycle.contract.ts"
COORDINATOR="${PLATFORM}/lifecycle/workspace-events-state-lifecycle-coordinator.ts"
LIFECYCLE_INDEX="${PLATFORM}/lifecycle/index.ts"

BASELINE="stage25-phase19-step01/PHASE19_BASELINE_MANIFEST.sha256"
CURRENT="/tmp/phase19-step16-current-src.sha256"
DELTA="/tmp/phase19-step16-baseline-delta.txt"

echo "======================================================================"
echo "COREI — STAGE-25 PHASE-19 STEP-16 — VALIDATION"
echo "======================================================================"

echo
echo "01 — REQUIRED STEP-16 FILE SURFACE"

for file in \
  "${CONTRACT}" \
  "${COORDINATOR}" \
  "${LIFECYCLE_INDEX}"
do
  test -s "${file}" \
    && echo "[PASS] ${file}" \
    || {
      echo "[FAIL] Missing or empty: ${file}"
      exit 1
    }
done

echo
echo "02 — TYPESCRIPT VALIDATION"

pnpm exec tsc --noEmit

echo "[PASS] TypeScript validation."

echo
echo "03 — PRODUCTION BUILD VALIDATION"

pnpm build

echo "[PASS] Production build validation."

echo
echo "04 — AUTHORITATIVE EVENT FAMILY"

EVENT_TYPES=(
  "workspace.opened"
  "workspace.activated"
  "workspace.deactivated"
  "workspace.closed"
  "window.opened"
  "window.focused"
  "window.closed"
  "layout.changed"
  "layout.restored"
  "workspace.persistence.completed"
  "workspace.recovery.started"
  "workspace.recovery.completed"
  "workspace.recovery.failed"
)

for event_type in "${EVENT_TYPES[@]}"; do
  grep -Fq "\"${event_type}\"" "${CONTRACT}" \
    && echo "[PASS] Lifecycle event: ${event_type}" \
    || {
      echo "[FAIL] Missing lifecycle event: ${event_type}"
      exit 1
    }
done

echo
echo "05 — EVENT / STATE ADAPTER PORTS"

for symbol in \
  "WorkspacePlatformEventPublicationPort" \
  "WorkspacePlatformStatePublicationPort"
do
  grep -Fq "${symbol}" "${CONTRACT}" \
    && echo "[PASS] Existing-platform adapter boundary: ${symbol}" \
    || {
      echo "[FAIL] Missing adapter boundary: ${symbol}"
      exit 1
    }
done

echo
echo "06 — COORDINATION CONTRACT"

for operation in \
  "coordinate" \
  "getState" \
  "getEvents" \
  "getSnapshot"
do
  grep -Fq "${operation}(" "${CONTRACT}" \
    && echo "[PASS] Coordination operation: ${operation}" \
    || {
      echo "[FAIL] Missing coordination operation: ${operation}"
      exit 1
    }
done

echo
echo "07 — DETERMINISTIC SEQUENCE COORDINATION"

grep -Fq \
  "this.sequence += 1" \
  "${COORDINATOR}" \
  || {
    echo "[FAIL] Deterministic sequence progression missing."
    exit 1
  }

grep -Fq \
  "sequence: this.sequence" \
  "${COORDINATOR}" \
  || {
    echo "[FAIL] Event sequence assignment missing."
    exit 1
  }

echo "[PASS] Deterministic event sequence coordination established."

echo
echo "08 — EVENT BEFORE STATE PUBLICATION ORDER"

EVENT_LINE="$(
  grep -n \
    'this.eventPublicationPort.publish' \
    "${COORDINATOR}" \
    | head -n1 \
    | cut -d: -f1
)"

STATE_LINE="$(
  grep -n \
    'this.statePublicationPort.publish' \
    "${COORDINATOR}" \
    | head -n1 \
    | cut -d: -f1
)"

if [[ -z "${EVENT_LINE}" || -z "${STATE_LINE}" ]]; then
  echo "[FAIL] Publication sequence surface missing."
  exit 1
fi

if (( EVENT_LINE < STATE_LINE )); then
  echo "[PASS] Event publication deterministically precedes state publication."
else
  echo "[FAIL] Event/state publication order is not deterministic."
  exit 1
fi

echo
echo "09 — SECOND GLOBAL STATE SYSTEM GUARD"

if grep -RInE \
  'createStore\(|configureStore\(|createSlice\(|zustand|redux|mobx|recoil|jotai' \
  "${PLATFORM}/lifecycle" \
  "${CONTRACT}"
then
  echo "[FAIL] Duplicate/global state-system implementation detected."
  exit 1
else
  echo "[PASS] No second global state system detected."
fi

echo
echo "10 — EVENT INFRASTRUCTURE REIMPLEMENTATION GUARD"

if grep -RInE \
  'class EventBus|class EventRegistry|class EventPlatform|new EventTarget|addEventListener\(|dispatchEvent\(' \
  "${PLATFORM}/lifecycle" \
  "${CONTRACT}"
then
  echo "[FAIL] Event infrastructure reimplementation detected."
  exit 1
else
  echo "[PASS] Existing Event Platform ownership preserved."
fi

echo
echo "11 — STATE INFRASTRUCTURE REIMPLEMENTATION GUARD"

if grep -RInE \
  'class StatePlatform|class StateRegistry|class GlobalStore|localStorage|sessionStorage|indexedDB' \
  "${PLATFORM}/lifecycle" \
  "${CONTRACT}"
then
  echo "[FAIL] State infrastructure reimplementation detected."
  exit 1
else
  echo "[PASS] Existing State Platform ownership preserved."
fi

echo
echo "12 — PROVIDER / FRAMEWORK INDEPENDENCE"

if grep -RInE \
  'from ['\''"](react|dockview|dockview-react|zustand|redux|mobx|recoil|jotai)' \
  "${PLATFORM}/lifecycle" \
  "${CONTRACT}"
then
  echo "[FAIL] Framework/provider coupling detected."
  exit 1
else
  echo "[PASS] Step-16 remains framework/provider independent."
fi

echo
echo "13 — STEP-17 DIAGNOSTICS EXECUTION GUARD"

if grep -RInE \
  'from ['\''"][^'\''"]*(diagnostic|observability|telemetry)|DiagnosticsPlatform|DiagnosticPlatform|ObservabilityPlatform|TelemetryPlatform|recordMetric\(|emitMetric\(|recordTelemetry\(|emitTelemetry\(' \
  "${PLATFORM}/lifecycle" \
  "${CONTRACT}"
then
  echo "[FAIL] Premature Step-17 diagnostics implementation detected."
  exit 1
else
  echo "[PASS] No premature Step-17 diagnostics implementation detected."
fi

echo
echo "14 — PHASE-20 BOUNDARY GUARD"

if grep -RInE \
  'PanelFramework|WidgetFramework|ViewFramework|VisualizationFramework|ChartFramework|GridFramework|TreeFramework|TerminalFramework|DashboardFramework' \
  "${PLATFORM}/lifecycle" \
  "${CONTRACT}"
then
  echo "[FAIL] Premature Phase-20 implementation detected."
  exit 1
else
  echo "[PASS] Phase-20 ownership remains blocked."
fi

echo
echo "15 — DIRECT BARREL SELF-DEPENDENCY GUARD"

if grep -nE \
  'from ["'\'']\.["'\'']|from ["'\'']\.\./contracts["'\'']' \
  "${CONTRACT}" \
  "${COORDINATOR}"
then
  echo "[FAIL] Internal barrel dependency detected."
  exit 1
else
  echo "[PASS] Internal Step-16 dependencies remain direct."
fi

echo
echo "16 — PUBLIC API VALIDATION"

grep -Fq \
  "WorkspaceEventsStateLifecycleContract" \
  "${PLATFORM}/contracts/index.ts"

grep -Fq \
  "WorkspaceEventsStateLifecycleCoordinator" \
  "${LIFECYCLE_INDEX}"

grep -Fq \
  "WorkspaceEventsStateLifecycleCoordinator" \
  "${PLATFORM}/index.ts"

echo "[PASS] Step-16 public API exposed."

echo
echo "17 — CIRCULAR DEPENDENCY VALIDATION"

pnpm exec madge \
  --circular \
  --extensions ts,tsx \
  "${PLATFORM}"

echo "[PASS] No Phase-19 circular dependency detected."

echo
echo "18 — DETERMINISTIC BASELINE DELTA"

find src \
  -type f \
  ! -name '*.map' \
  ! -name '*.tsbuildinfo' \
  -print0 \
  | sort -z \
  | xargs -0 sha256sum \
  > "${CURRENT}"

comm -3 \
  <(sort "${BASELINE}") \
  <(sort "${CURRENT}") \
  > "${DELTA}"

cat "${DELTA}"

UNEXPECTED_DELTA="$(
  grep -vF \
    "src/window-workspace-platform/" \
    "${DELTA}" \
    | grep -vF \
      "src/workbench/components/WorkspaceHost.tsx" \
    | grep -vF \
      "src/workbench/integration/WindowWorkspaceRuntimeProvider.tsx" \
    | grep -vF \
      "src/workbench/integration/index.ts" \
    | grep -vF \
      "src/workbench/index.ts" \
    || true
)"

if [[ -n "$(printf '%s' "${UNEXPECTED_DELTA}" | tr -d '[:space:]')" ]]; then
  echo "[FAIL] Unexpected source delta detected:"
  printf '%s\n' "${UNEXPECTED_DELTA}"
  exit 1
else
  echo "[PASS] Source delta restricted to Phase-19 and authorized Step-15 Workbench integration files."
fi

echo
echo "19 — PROTECTED KERNEL BOUNDARY CHECK"

PROTECTED_PREFIXES=(
  "src/kernel/"
  "src/platform/"
  "src/platform-runtime/"
  "src/runtime/"
  "src/platform-bootstrap/"
  "src/platform-configuration/"
  "src/platform-registry/"
  "src/providers/"
  "src/capabilities/integration/"
  "src/foundation/workspace/"
  "src/workspaces/"
  "src/events/"
  "src/state/"
)

for prefix in "${PROTECTED_PREFIXES[@]}"; do
  if grep -Fq "${prefix}" "${DELTA}"; then
    echo "[FAIL] Protected Kernel boundary delta detected: ${prefix}"
    exit 1
  fi
done

echo "[PASS] Existing Kernel Event and State Platforms remain unmodified."

echo
echo "20 — SHELL SOURCE PRESERVATION"

if grep -Fq \
  "src/shell/" \
  "${DELTA}"
then
  echo "[FAIL] Step-16 unexpectedly modified the frozen Shell."
  exit 1
else
  echo "[PASS] Frozen Institutional Shell source preserved."
fi

echo
echo "21 — BROWSER VISIBILITY CHANGE CLASSIFICATION"

if grep -Fq \
  "INTENTIONAL_BROWSER_VISUAL_CHANGES=0" \
  /tmp/stage25-phase19-step16-implement-console.txt
then
  echo "[PASS] Step-16 classified as no intentional browser visual change."
else
  echo "[FAIL] Browser visibility classification missing."
  exit 1
fi

echo

echo "  - Application loads successfully."
echo "  - Existing Institutional Shell remains visible."
echo "  - Existing center WorkspaceHost remains visible."
echo "  - Existing Workbench presentation remains visually unchanged."
echo "  - No new event UI is expected."
echo "  - No new state UI is expected."
echo "  - No diagnostics UI is expected."
echo "  - Any unexpected visual change must be treated as a regression."

echo
echo "======================================================================"
echo "PHASE19_STEP16_VALIDATION=PASS"
echo "======================================================================"
EOF

chmod +x /tmp/stage25-phase19-step16-validate.sh

bash -n /tmp/stage25-phase19-step16-validate.sh \
  && echo "[PASS] Step-16 validation harness shell syntax."

/tmp/stage25-phase19-step16-validate.sh \
  2>&1 | tee /tmp/stage25-phase19-step16-validation-console.txt
